## The ticket

Replace the ad-hoc cache fingerprint in `llm/llm.py` with an explicit, versioned run identity. Add a new top-level module `src/bespokelabs/curator/run_identity.py` (top-level so `db.py` can import it without a cycle) exporting `RUN_IDENTITY_VERSION: int = 3`, `RUN_IDENTITY_FILENAME: str = "run_identity.json"`; an exception family `RunIdentityError(RuntimeError)` with subclasses `RunIdentityMismatch(path, expected_run_hash, found_run_hash, mismatched_components)` and `CachedResponseMismatch(cache_dir, field, expected, found)` (each argument kept as a same-named attribute); frozen dataclasses `RunIdentity(run_hash, digest, identity_version, cache_enabled, components)`, `RunStamp(identity_version, run_hash, digest, components, created_at, updated_at)` with `to_dict()`/`from_dict()`, and `RunDirectoryCheck(status, stamp, previous_version)`; and functions `compute_run_identity(llm, dataset_hash, *, cache_enabled: bool = True, ...)` (duck-typed on the object it is handed — no `isinstance`, no import of `LLM`), `write_run_stamp(run_cache_dir, identity, *, now: str, created_at: Optional[str] = None)`, `read_run_stamp(run_cache_dir) -> Optional[RunStamp]`, `reconcile_run_directory(run_cache_dir, identity, *, now: str) -> RunDirectoryCheck`. The `digest` is a 16-char lowercase `xxh64` hexdigest over a canonical, key-sorted, version-tagged serialisation of the identity components; `run_hash` for a normal cached run is `f"v3-{digest}"` and is the cache directory name. The stamp file holds exactly `identity_version`, `run_hash`, `digest`, `components`, `created_at`, `updated_at` as `json.dumps(..., indent=2, sort_keys=True)` plus a trailing newline (`created_at` preserved when supplied, `updated_at` always `now`); `read_run_stamp` returns `None` for an absent, unparseable or incomplete file. `reconcile_run_directory` returns `status` in `"created" | "adopted" | "upgraded" | "matched"` — create/stamp a missing or empty directory, adopt an unstamped non-empty one, rewrite an older-version stamp keeping its `created_at`, refresh `updated_at` on an exact match; `previous_version` is `None` for a create and for an adopt, the old integer for an upgrade, and `3` for a match — and raises `RunIdentityMismatch` (deleting nothing) when a same-version stamp disagrees or a newer `identity_version` is found, with `mismatched_components` the alphabetically sorted differing component keys, or `("identity_version",)`. Move `_get_function_hash` verbatim out of `llm/llm.py` into the new module and re-export it there; delete `_hash_fingerprint` in favour of `LLM._run_identity(dataset_hash, *, cache_enabled: bool, ...)`; add `LLM.backend` and `LLM.backend_params` properties; have `LLM.__call__` reconcile the run directory before using it and `LLM._get_cached_response` re-raise `RunIdentityError` while still returning `None` (with the existing warning) for anything else. In `db.py` add `RUNS_COLUMNS` including new `parse_func` and `identity_version` columns, make `validate_schema()` migrate missing columns forward via `ALTER TABLE` and return the tuple of names it added (unexpected columns stay fatal), and have `store_metadata()` write `parse_func` and `identity_version`, update `session_id` only when the incoming value is not `None`, and return `"inserted"` or `"updated"`. In `curator_response.py` add a `run_identity: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None` field declared last, emit it from `to_dict()`, and give `load(cls, cache_dir, dataset, *, verify: bool = True)` a check of the recorded `"dataset"` block (`fingerprint`, `size`, `columns`) against the dataset it is handed, raising `CachedResponseMismatch` on the first differing field and skipping the check when the key is absent or `verify=False`. Finally, `BaseRequestProcessor.run()` sets `self._is_cached_dataset = False` as its first statement.

</problem>

\\"cancel_batches\\"):\\n                raise ValueError(\\"batch_cancel can only be used with batch mode\\")\\n\\n            run_in_event_loop(\\n                self._request_processor.cancel_batches(\\n                    working_dir=run_cache_dir, dataset=dataset, prompt_formatter=self.prompt_formatter, auto_confirm=batch_cancel_auto_confirm\\n                )\\n            )\\n            return CuratorResponse(dataset=dataset, batch_mode=self.batch_mode, cache_dir=run_cache_dir)\\n\\n        elif batch_cancel:\\n            logger.warning(\\"You set batch_cancel=True but you\'re not in batch mode. Ignoring batch_cancel.\\")\\n\\n        metadata_db_path = os.path.join(curator_cache_dir, \\"metadata.db\\")\\n        metadata_db = MetadataDB(metadata_db_path)\\n        self._request_processor.viewer_client = Client()\\n\\n        # Get the source code of the prompt function\\n        prompt_func_source = _get_function_source(self.prompt_formatter.prompt_func)\\n        if self.prompt_formatter.parse_func is not None:\\n            parse_func_source = _get_function_source(self.prompt_formatter.parse_func)\\n        else:\\n            parse_func_source = \\"\\"\\n\\n        metadata_dict = {\\n            \\"timestamp\\": datetime.now().isoformat(),\\n            \\"dataset_hash\\": dataset_hash,\\n            \\"prompt_func\\": prompt_func_source,\\n            \\"parse_func\\": parse_func_source,\\n            \\"model_name\\": self.prompt_formatter.model_name,\\n            \\"response_format\\": (str(self.prompt_formatter.response_format.model_json_schema()) if self.prompt_formatter.response_format else \\"text\\"),\\n            \\"run_hash\\": fingerprint,\\n            \\"batch_mode\\": self.batch_mode,\\n        }\\n\\n        existing_session_id = metadata_db.get_existing_session_id(metadata_dict[\\"run_hash\\"])\\n        existing_viewer_sync = metadata_db.check_existing_hosted_sync(metadata_dict[\\"run_hash\\"])\\n        if not existing_viewer_sync and existing_session_id:\\n            session_id = self._request_processor.viewer_client.create_session(metadata_dict)\\n        else:\\n            session_id = self._request_processor.viewer_client.create_session(metadata_dict, session_id=existing_session_id)\\n\\n        metadata_dict[\\"session_id\\"] = session_id\\n        metadata_dict[\\"is_hosted_viewer_synced\\"] = False\\n        metadata_db.store_metadata(metadata_dict)\\n\\n        parse_func_hash = _get_function_hash(self.prompt_formatter.parse_func)\\n        dataset = self._request_processor.run(\\n            dataset=dataset,\\n            working_dir=run_cache_dir,\\n            parse_func_hash=parse_func_hash,\\n            prompt_formatter=self.prompt_formatter,\\n        )\\n        viewer_url = self._request_processor.viewer_client.curator_viewer_url\\n\\n        if existing_session_id is not None and existing_viewer_sync is False:\\n            msg = (\\n                f\\"There was a previous run with the same run hash ({metadata_dict[\'run_hash\']}) without the CURATOR_VIEWER flag enabled, \\"\\n                \\"and CURATOR_VIEWER flag is enabled for this run. This means that the Curator Viewer is potentially inconsistent with local data.\\"\\n                \\"Pushing the full dataset to the Curator Viewer to ensure full consistency.\\"\\n            )\\n            if self._request_processor.viewer_client.hosted:\\n                logger.warning(msg)\\n                from bespokelabs.curator.utils import push_to_viewer\\n\\n                viewer_url = push_to_viewer(dataset, session_id=session_id)\\n\\n        if self._request_processor.viewer_client.hosted:\\n            metadata_db.update_sync_viewer_flag(metadata_dict[\\"run_hash\\"], True)\\n\\n        # Get tracker information\\n        if not self._request_processor._is_cached_dataset:\\n            # Create response object with tracker info\\n            tracker = self._request_processor.tracker\\n            response = CuratorResponse(\\n                cache_dir=run_cache_dir,\\n                dataset=dataset,\\n                batch_mode=self.batch_mode,\\n                viewer_url=viewer_url,\\n                failed_requests_path=Path(os.path.join(run_cache_dir, \\"failed_requests.jsonl\\"))\\n                if os.path.exists(os.path.join(run_cache_dir, \\"failed_requests.jsonl\\"))\\n                else None,\\n                model_name=self.prompt_formatter.model_name,\\n                metadata=metadata_dict,\\n            )\\n            response.update_tracker_stats(tracker)\\n        else:\\n            # Create minimal response object if no tracker\\n            response = self._get_cached_response(run_cache_dir, dataset)\\n            if response is None:\\n                response = CuratorResponse(\\n                    dataset=dataset,\\n                    cache_dir=run_cache_dir,\\n                    viewer_url=viewer_url,\\n                    batch_mode=self.batch_mode,\\n                    failed_requests_path=Path(os.path.join(run_cache_dir, \\"failed_requests.jsonl\\"))\\n                    if os.path.exists(os.path.join(run_cache_dir, \\"failed_requests.jsonl\\"))\\n                    else None,\\n                    model_name=self.prompt_formatter.model_name,\\n                    metadata=metadata_dict,\\n                )\\n\\n        # Save the response if cache directory is specified\\n        response.save(run_cache_dir)\\n\\n        return response\\n\\n\\ndef _get_function_hash(func) -> str:\\n    # setting recursion limit to avoid random recursion limit error\\n    # todo: understand why this is happening and fix it\\n    import sys\\n\\n    sys.setrecursionlimit(10000)\\n\\n    \\"\\"\\"Get a hash of a function\'s source code.\\"\\"\\"\\n    if func is None:\\n        return xxh64(\\"\\").hexdigest()\\n\\n    # Remove parameter annotations to avoid dill complaining about pickling\\n    # pydantic BaseModel: https://github.com/bespokelabsai/curator/issues/229.\\n    # For class/instance methods, get the underlying function\\n    if hasattr(func, \\"__func__\\"):\\n        func = func.__func__\\n\\n    # Clear annotations if they exist\\n    if hasattr(func, \\"__annotations__\\"):\\n        func.__annotations__ = {}\\n\\n    file = BytesIO()\\n\\n    from datasets.utils._dill import Pickler\\n\\n    try:\\n        Pickler(file, recurse=True).dump(func)\\n    except TypeError:\\n        logger.debug(\\"Failed to recursive pickle function, trying non-recursive\\")\\n        Pickler(file, recurse=False).dump(func)\\n\\n    return xxh64(file.getvalue()).hexdigest()\\n\\n\\ndef _get_function_source(func) -> str:\\n    \\"\\"\\"Get the source code of a function.\\n\\n    Purpose of this function is that during Python interpreter (REPL),\\n    `inspect.getsource` will fail with an OSError because functions defined in the\\n    interpreter don\'t have an associated source file. We have to use this wrapper\\n    to gracefully handle this case.\\n    \\"\\"\\"\\n    try:\\n        return inspect.getsource(func)\\n    except OSError:\\n        return \\"\\"\\n\\n\\ndef _remove_none_values(d: dict) -> dict:\\n    \\"\\"\\"Remove all None values from a dictionary.\\"\\"\\"\\n    return {k: v for k, v in d.items() if v is not None}\\n\\n\\ndef _is_message_list(list: list) -> bool:\\n    \\"\\"\\"Check if a list is a list of messages.\\"\\"\\"\\n    return all(isinstance(item, dict) and \\"role\\" in item and \\"content\\" in item for item in list)\\n\\n\\ndef _convert_to_dataset(iterable: Iterable | CuratorResponse) -> \\"Dataset\\":\\n    \\"\\"\\"Convert an iterable to a Dataset.\\n\\n    The prompt is expected to be a prompt string or a list of messages.\\n    It will be stored with the key \'__internal_prompt\' internally.\\n    \\"\\"\\"\\n    if isinstance(iterable, CuratorResponse):\\n        return iterable.dataset\\n\\n    if isinstance(iterable, str) or _is_message_list(iterable):\\n        # A single string or list of messages is converted to a dataset with a single row\\n        dataset = Dataset.from_list([{_INTERNAL_PROMPT_KEY: iterable}])\\n    elif not isinstance(iterable, Dataset) and iterable is not None:\\n        # Wrap the iterable in a generator, the prompt is expected to be a prompt string or a list of messages\\n        def wrapped_iterable():\\n            for input in iterable:\\n                if isinstance(input, str) or _is_message_list(input):\\n                    yield {_INTERNAL_PROMPT_KEY: input}\\n                else:\\n                    yield input\\n\\n        dataset = Dataset.from_generator(wrapped_iterable)\\n    else:\\n        dataset = iterable\\n\\n    return dataset",\n  "error": "",\n  "base64_image": null,\n  "system": null\n}', annotations=None, meta=None)] structuredContent=None isError=False

meta=None content=[TextContent(type='text', text='{\n  "output": "\\"\\"\\"Database class for storing metadata for Bella runs.\\"\\"\\"\\n\\nimport os\\nimport sqlite3\\n\\n\\nclass MetadataDB:\\n    \\"\\"\\"Database class for storing Bella run metadata.\\"\\"\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, db_path: str):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Initialize the MetadataDB with a given database path.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            db_path: Path to the SQLite database file.\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        self.db_path = db_path\\n\\n    def _get_current_schema(self) -> list:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Get the current schema of the runs table from the database.\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            list: List of tuples containing column information.\\n                  Each tuple contains (cid, name, type, notnull, dflt_value, pk)\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:\\n            cursor = conn.cursor()\\n            cursor.execute(\\"PRAGMA table_info(runs)\\")\\n            schema_info = cursor.fetchall()\\n        return schema_info\\n\\n    def validate_schema(self):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Validate that the current database schema matches the expected schema.\\n\\n        Raises:\\n            RuntimeError: If there is a mismatch between the current schema and expected schema,\\n                        with instructions to clear the cache.\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        expected_columns = [\\n            \\"run_hash\\",\\n            \\"session_id\\",\\n            \\"dataset_hash\\",\\n            \\"prompt_func\\",\\n            \\"model_name\\",\\n            \\"response_format\\",\\n            \\"batch_mode\\",\\n            \\"created_time\\",\\n            \\"last_edited_time\\",\\n            \\"is_hosted_viewer_synced\\",\\n            # Cost management columns\\n            \\"total_cost_milli_dollars\\",\\n            \\"total_requests\\",\\n            \\"total_prompt_tokens\\",\\n            \\"total_completion_tokens\\",\\n        ]\\n        current_info = self._get_current_schema()\\n        current_columns = [col[1] for col in current_info]  # col[1] = column name\\n\\n        if set(current_columns) != set(expected_columns):\\n            msg = (\\n                \\"Detected a mismatch between the local DB schema and the expected schema. \\"\\n                \\"Please clear your cache with `rm -rf ~/.cache/curator` or \\"\\n                \\"`rm -rf $CURATOR_CACHE_DIR` if set.\\"\\n            )\\n            raise RuntimeError(msg)\\n\\n    def store_metadata(self, metadata: dict):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Store metadata about a Bella run in the database.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            metadata: Dictionary containing run metadata with keys:\\n                - timestamp: ISO format timestamp\\n                - dataset_hash: Unique hash of input dataset\\n                - prompt_func: Source code of prompt function\\n                - model_name: Name of model used\\n                - response_format: JSON schema of response format\\n                - run_hash: Unique hash identifying the run\\n                - batch_mode: Boolean indicating batch mode or online mode (True = batch, False = online)\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(self.db_path), exist_ok=True)\\n        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:\\n            cursor = conn.cursor()\\n            # IMPORTANT: If you modify the CREATE TABLE schema below,\\n            # you must update the expected_columns list in validate_schema()\\n            # to match the new schema. Otherwise, schema validation will fail.\\n            cursor.execute(\\n                \\"\\"\\"\\n                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS runs (\\n                    run_hash TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\\n                    session_id TEXT,\\n                    dataset_hash TEXT,\\n                    prompt_func TEXT,\\n                    model_name TEXT,\\n                    response_format TEXT,\\n                    batch_mode BOOLEAN,\\n                    created_time TEXT,\\n                    last_edited_time TEXT,\\n                    is_hosted_viewer_synced BOOLEAN,\\n\\n                    total_cost_milli_dollars TEXT,\\n                    total_requests TEXT,\\n                    total_prompt_tokens TEXT,\\n                    total_completion_tokens TEXT\\n                )\\n                \\"\\"\\"\\n            )\\n            self.validate_schema()\\n\\n            # Check if run_hash exists\\n            cursor.execute(\\n                \\"SELECT run_hash FROM runs WHERE run_hash = ?\\",\\n                (metadata[\\"run_hash\\"],),\\n            )\\n            existing_run = cursor.fetchone()\\n\\n            if existing_run:\\n                # Update last_edited_time for existing entry\\n                cursor.execute(\\n                    \\"\\"\\"\\n                    UPDATE runs\\n                    SET last_edited_time = ?\\n                    WHERE run_hash = ?\\n                    \\"\\"\\",\\n                    (metadata[\\"timestamp\\"], metadata[\\"run_hash\\"]),\\n                )\\n            else:\\n                # Insert new entry\\n                cursor.execute(\\n                    \\"\\"\\"\\n                    INSERT INTO runs (\\n                        run_hash, session_id, dataset_hash, prompt_func, model_name,\\n                        response_format, batch_mode, created_time, last_edited_time, is_hosted_viewer_synced\\n                    ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\\n                    \\"\\"\\",\\n                    (\\n                        metadata[\\"run_hash\\"],\\n                        metadata[\\"session_id\\"],\\n                        metadata[\\"dataset_hash\\"],\\n                        metadata[\\"prompt_func\\"],\\n                        metadata[\\"model_name\\"],\\n                        metadata[\\"response_format\\"],\\n                        metadata[\\"batch_mode\\"],\\n                        metadata[\\"timestamp\\"],\\n                        metadata[\\"timestamp\\"],  # last_edited_time same as created_time initially\\n                        metadata[\\"is_hosted_viewer_synced\\"],\\n                    ),\\n                )\\n            conn.commit()\\n\\n    def get_existing_session_id(self, run_hash: str):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Get existing session id from previous run.\\"\\"\\"\\n        return self._get_metadata(run_hash, \\"session_id\\")\\n\\n    def _get_metadata(self, run_hash: str, column: str):\\n        try:\\n            with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:\\n                cursor = conn.cursor()\\n                cursor.execute(\\n                    f\\"SELECT {column} FROM runs WHERE run_hash = ?\\",\\n                    (run_hash,),\\n                )\\n                fetch = cursor.fetchone()\\n                if fetch:\\n                    return fetch[0]\\n\\n        except Exception:\\n            return None\\n\\n    def check_existing_hosted_sync(self, run_hash: str) -> bool:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Check if the run is already hosted on the viewer.\\"\\"\\"\\n        return bool(self._get_metadata(run_hash, \\"is_hosted_viewer_synced\\"))\\n\\n    def update_sync_viewer_flag(self, run_hash: str, hosted: bool):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Update the hosted_viewer boolean for a run.\\"\\"\\"\\n        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:\\n            cursor = conn.cursor()\\n            cursor.execute(\\n                \\"UPDATE runs SET is_hosted_viewer_synced = ? WHERE run_hash = ?\\",\\n                (hosted, run_hash),\\n            )\\n            conn.commit()",\n  "error": "",\n  "base64_image": null,\n  "system": null\n}', annotations=None, meta=None)] structuredContent=None isError=False

If both instances have input/output costs\\n        per million tokens, those are also added.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            other (CostInfo): Another CostInfo instance whose values will be added.\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        self.total_cost += other.total_cost\\n        if self.input_cost_per_million and other.input_cost_per_million:\\n            self.input_cost_per_million += other.input_cost_per_million\\n        if self.output_cost_per_million and other.output_cost_per_million:\\n            self.output_cost_per_million += other.output_cost_per_million\\n\\n        self.projected_remaining_cost += other.projected_remaining_cost\\n\\n\\n@dataclass\\nclass RequestStats:\\n    \\"\\"\\"Request statistics.\\"\\"\\"\\n\\n    total: int = 0\\n    succeeded: int = 0\\n    failed: int = 0\\n    in_progress: int = 0\\n    cached: int = 0\\n\\n    def add(self, other: \\"RequestStats\\"):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Add request statistics from another RequestStats instance.\\n\\n        This method adds all request counts (total, succeeded, failed, in_progress,\\n        and cached) from another RequestStats instance to this one.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            other (RequestStats): Another RequestStats instance whose values will be added.\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        self.total += other.total\\n        self.succeeded += other.succeeded\\n        self.failed += other.failed\\n        self.in_progress += other.in_progress\\n        self.cached += other.cached\\n\\n\\n@dataclass\\nclass PerformanceStats:\\n    \\"\\"\\"Performance statistics.\\"\\"\\"\\n\\n    total_time: float = 0.0\\n    requests_per_minute: float = 0.0\\n    input_tokens_per_minute: float = 0.0\\n    output_tokens_per_minute: float = 0.0\\n    max_concurrent_requests: int = 0\\n\\n    def add(self, other: \\"PerformanceStats\\"):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Add performance statistics from another PerformanceStats instance.\\n\\n        This method adds all performance metrics (total time, requests per minute,\\n        input/output tokens per minute, and max concurrent requests) from another\\n        PerformanceStats instance to this one.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            other (PerformanceStats): Another PerformanceStats instance whose values will be added.\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        self.total_time += other.total_time\\n        self.requests_per_minute += other.requests_per_minute\\n        self.input_tokens_per_minute += other.input_tokens_per_minute\\n        self.output_tokens_per_minute += other.output_tokens_per_minute\\n        self.max_concurrent_requests += other.max_concurrent_requests\\n\\n\\n@dataclass\\nclass CuratorResponse:\\n    \\"\\"\\"Response from Curator LLM processing.\\n\\n    This class encapsulates all the information about a Curator processing run,\\n    including the dataset, failed requests, and various statistics.\\n    \\"\\"\\"\\n\\n    # Core data\\n    dataset: Dataset\\n    cache_dir: Optional[str] = None\\n    failed_requests_path: Optional[Path] = None\\n    viewer_url: Optional[str] = None\\n    batch_mode: bool = False\\n\\n    # TokenUsage = field(default_factory=lambda: TokenUsage(0, 0, 0))\\n    cost_info: CostInfo = field(default_factory=lambda: CostInfo(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0))\\n    request_stats: RequestStats = field(default_factory=lambda: RequestStats(0, 0, 0, 0, 0))\\n    performance_stats: PerformanceStats = field(default_factory=lambda: PerformanceStats(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0))\\n\\n    # Additional metadata\\n    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)\\n\\n    def __post_init__(self):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Post-initialization hook.\\"\\"\\"\\n        if self.metadata is None:\\n            self.metadata = {}\\n\\n    def __getattr__(self, name):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Get an attribute from the response.\\n\\n        This method is called when an attribute is not found in the normal attribute being accessed.\\n\\n        Raises:\\n            AttributeError: Always raises this error with a deprecation warning.\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        warnings.warn(\\"Warning: Huggingface response from the curator LLM api is deprecated, please check out the `CuratorResponse`\\", stacklevel=2)\\n        raise AttributeError(f\\"\'{self.__class__.__name__}\' object has no attribute \'{name}\'\\")\\n\\n    def update_tracker_stats(self, tracker) -> None:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Update the response with statistics from a tracker.\\n\\n        This method updates various statistics in the response object based on\\n        the provided tracker\'s data, including token usage, costs, request stats,\\n        and performance metrics.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            tracker: The tracker object containing statistics about the processing run.\\n                    Can be either an OfflineStatusTracker or None.\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        if isinstance(tracker, if isinstance(tracker.max_tokens_per_minute, BaseModel):\\n                max_tokens_per_minute = tracker.max_tokens_per_minute.model_dump()\\n            else:\\n                max_tokens_per_minute = tracker.max_tokens_per_minute\\n            self.max_requests_per_minute = tracker.max_requests_per_minute\\n            self.max_tokens_per_minute = max_tokens_per_minute\\n\\n        # Update token usage\\n        self.token_usage = TokenUsage(input=tracker.total_prompt_tokens, output=tracker.total_completion_tokens, total=tracker.total_tokens)\\n\\n        # Update cost information\\n        self.cost_info = CostInfo(\\n            total_cost=tracker.total_cost,\\n            input_cost_per_million=tracker.input_cost_per_million,\\n            output_cost_per_million=tracker.output_cost_per_million,\\n            projected_remaining_cost=tracker.projected_remaining_cost,\\n        )\\n\\n        # Update request statistics\\n        if self.batch_mode:\\n            total = tracker.n_total_requests\\n tracker.total_requests\\n            succeeded = tracker.num_tasks_succeeded\\n            failed = tracker.num_tasks_failed\\n            in_progress = tracker.num_tasks_in_progress\\n            cached = tracker.num_tasks_already_completed\\n\\n        self.request_stats = RequestStats(total=total, succeeded=succeeded, failed=failed, in_progress=in_progress, cached=cached)\\n\\n        # requests_per_minute=self.request_stats.succeeded / max(0.001, (time.time() - tracker.start_time) / 60),\\n            input_tokens_per_minute=self.token_usage.input / max(0.001, (time.time() - tracker.start_time) / 60),\\n            output_tokens_per_minute=self.token_usage.output / max(0.001, (time.time() - tracker.start_time) / 60),\\n            max_concurrent_requests=tracker.max_concurrent_requests_seen if not self.batch_mode else 0,\\n        )\\n\\n    def get_failed_requests(self) -> Iterable[Dict[str, Any]]:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Get an iterator over failed requests.\\n\\n        This method reads the failed requests from a JSONL file and yields\\n        each request as a dictionary. If no failed requests file exists,\\n        returns an empty iterator.\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            Iterable[Dict[str, Any]]: An iterator that yields dictionaries\\n                                     containing failed request data.\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        if self.failed_requests_path is None or not self.failed_requests_path.exists():\\n            return iter([])\\n\\n        def _iter_failed_requests():\\n            with open(self.failed_requests_path, \\"r\\") as f:\\n                for line in f:\\n                    yield json.loads(line)\\n\\n        return _iter_failed_requests()\\n\\n    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Convert the response to a dictionary.\\n\\n        This method creates a dictionary representation of the CuratorResponse\\n        object, including dataset information, model details, statistics,\\n        and metadata.\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            Dict[str, Any]: A dictionary containing all the response data\\n                           in a serializable format.\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        return {\\n            \\"dataset\\": {\\n                \\"fingerprint\\": self.dataset._fingerprint,\\n                \\"size\\": len(self.dataset),\\n                \\"columns\\": list(self.dataset.column_names),\\n            },\\n            \\"failed_requests_path\\": str(self.failed_requests_path) if self.failed_requests_path else None,\\n            \\"model_name\\": self.model_name,\\n            \\"max_requests_per_minute\\": self.max_requests_per_minute,\\n            \\"max_tokens_per_minute\\": self.max_tokens_per_minute,\\n            \\"token_usage\\": {\\n                \\"input\\": self.token_usage.input,\\n                \\"output\\": \\"cached\\": self.request_stats.cached,\\n            },\\n            \\"performance_stats\\": {\\n                \\"total_time\\": self.performance_stats.total_time,\\n                \\"requests_per_minute\\": self.performance_stats.requests_per_minute,\\n                \\"input_tokens_per_minute\\": self.performance_stats.input_tokens_per_minute,\\n                \\"output_tokens_per_minute\\": self.performance_stats.output_tokens_per_minute,\\n                \\"max_concurrent_requests\\": self.performance_stats.max_concurrent_requests,\\n            },\\n            \\"metadata\\": self.metadata,\\n        }\\n\\n    def save(self, cache_dir: Union[str, Path]) -> None:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Save the response to a cache directory.\\n\\n        This method saves the response data to a JSON file in the specified\\n        cache directory. The directory will be created if it doesn\'t exist.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            cache_dir (Union[str, Path]): Directory where the response will be saved.\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        cache_dir = Path(cache_dir)\\n        cache_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\\n\\n        # Save response.json\\n        with open(cache_dir / \\"response.json\\", \\"w\\") as f:\\n            json.dump(self.to_dict(), f, indent=2)\\n\\n    @classmethod\\n    def load(cls, cache_dir: Union[str, Path], dataset: Dataset) -> \\"CuratorResponse\\":\\n        \\"\\"\\"Load a response from a cache directory.\\n\\n        This class method creates a new CuratorResponse instance from saved\\n        data in the cache directory. successful_indices = set(dataset[\\"__original_row_idx\\"])\\n            except Exception as e:\\n                logger.warning(f\\"Error reading dataset file to extract successful indices: {e}\\")\\n\\n            # Get all request files\\n            request_files = glob.glob(os.path.join(self.working_dir, \\"requests_*.jsonl\\"))\\n\\n            # Write all requests that don\'t have corresponding entries in the dataset\\n            with open(failed_requests_file, \\"w\\") as failed_file:\\n                for request_file in request_files:\\n                    with open(request_file, \\"r\\") as f:\\n                        for line in f:\\n                            try:\\n                                request_data = json.loads(line.strip())\\n                                original_idx = request_data.get(\\"original_row_idx\\")\\n                                if original_idx is not None and original_idx not in successful_indices:\\n                                    failed_file.write(line)\\n                            except json.JSONDecodeError:\\n                                continue\\n\\n            logger.info(f\\"Created file with failed requests at {failed_requests_file}\\")\\n\\n            # number of responses matches number of requests\\n            request_files = glob.glob(os.path.join(self.working_dir, \\"requests_*.jsonl\\"))\\n            n_requests = 0\\n            for request_file in request_files:\\n                n_requests += count_lines(request_file)\\n\\n            if n_requests != total_responses_count:\\n                logger.warning(\\n                    f\\"{n_requests - total_responses_count} requests do not have responses. \\"\\n                    f\\"n_requests is {n_requests} and n_responses is {total_responses_count}\\"\\n                )\\n                if self.config.require_all_responses:\\n                    os.remove(dataset_file)\\n                    raise ValueError(\\"Some requests do not have responses and require_all_responses is True.\\")\\n\\n        return self._load_from_dataset_file(dataset_file)\\n\\n    def _load_from_dataset_file(self, dataset_file: str) -> \\"Dataset\\":\\n        from datasets import Dataset\\n\\n        d = Dataset.from_file(dataset_file)\\n        d = d.sort(\\"__original_row_idx\\")\\n        d = d.remove_columns(\\"__original_row_idx\\")\\n\\n        push_to_hub = functools.partial(BaseRequestProcessor.push_to_hub, dataset=d, _push_to_hub=d.push_to_hub)\\n        d.push_to_hub = push_to_hub\\n\\n        return d\\n\\n    @staticmethod\\n    def push_to_hub(repo_id: str, dataset=None, _push_to_hub=None, **kwargs):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Push the dataset to the hub and create a dataset card.\\"\\"\\"\\n        from huggingface_hub import DatasetCard\\n\\n        _push_to_hub(repo_id, **kwargs)\\n        card = DatasetCard(\\n            HUGGINGFACE_CARD_TEMPLATE.format(\\n                dataset_name=repo_id.split(\\"/\\")[-1],\\n                repo_id=repo_id,\\n                sample=json.dumps(dataset[0], indent=4),\\n            )\\n        )\\n        card.push_to_hub(repo_id)\\n\\n    def _get_validated_response(self, line: str) -> tuple[GenericResponse | None, bool]:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Check if a response is valid or has errors.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            line: The line to process into a GenericResponse.\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            response: The response if it is valid, None otherwise.\\n            is_valid: True if the response is valid, False otherwise.\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        response = None\\n        try:\\n            response = GenericResponse.model_validate_json(line)\\n            row_id = response.generic_request.original_row_idx\\n            if response.response_errors:\\n                logger.debug(f\\"Request {row_id} previously failed due to errors: {response.response_errors}, removing from output and will retry\\")\\n                return None, False\\n            if response.response_message is None:\\n                logger.debug(f\\"Request {row_id} previously failed due to no response. Removing from output and will retry.\\")\\n                return None, False\\n        except (json.JSONDecodeError, ValidationError):\\n            logger.warning(f\\"Skipping response due to error parsing line: {line}\\")\\n            return None, False\\n\\n        return response, True\\n\\n    def validate_existing_response_file(self, response_file: str) -> t.Union[set[int], int]:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Parse an existing response file to identify completed requests and removes failed requests.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            response_file: Path to the response file to parse\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            set[int]: Set of completed request IDs that were already successfully processed\\n            int: Number of completed parsed responses\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        if not os.path.exists(response_file):\\n            return set(), 0\\n\\n        completed_request_ids = set()\\n        failed_request_ids = set()\\n        completed_parsed_responses = 0\\n        parsing_error_responses = 0\\n        logger.info(f\\"Resuming progress by reading existing file: {response_file}\\")\\n        logger.debug(f\\"Removing all failed requests from {response_file} so they can be retried\\")\\n        temp_filepath = response_file + \\".temp\\"\\n\\n        with open(response_file, \\"r\\") as input_file, open(temp_filepath, \\"w\\") as output_file:\\n            for line in input_file:\\n                response, is_valid = self._get_validated_response(line)\\n                if not response:\\n                    parsing_error_responses += 1\\n                    continue\\n                row_id = response.generic_request.original_row_idx\\n                if response.parsed_response_message:\\n                    completed_parsed_responses += len(response.parsed_response_message)\\n                if is_valid:\\n                    completed_request_ids.add(row_id)\\n                    output_file.write(line)\\n                else:\\n                    failed_request_ids.add(row_id)\\n\\n        logger.info(\\n            f\\"Found {len(completed_request_ids)} successful requests and {len(failed_request_ids)} \\"\\n            f\\"previously failed requests and {parsing_error_responses} parsing errors in {response_file}\\"\\n        )\\n        os.replace(temp_filepath, response_file)\\n\\n        return completed_request_ids, completed_parsed_responses\\n\\n    def read_metadata_file(self, request_file: str) -> int:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Read the number of jobs from the metadata file.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            request_file: Path to the request file to get metadata for\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            int: Number of total batch requests\\n\\n        Raises:\\n            ValueError: If metadata file is missing or invalid\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        metadata_file = request_file.replace(\\"requests_\\", \\"metadata_\\").replace(\\".jsonl\\", \\".json\\")\\n\\n        if not os.path.exists(metadata_file):\\n            raise ValueError(f\\"Metadata file not found: {metadata_file}\\")\\n\\n        try:\\n            with open(metadata_file, \\"r\\") as f:\\n                metadata = json.load(f)\\n                return metadata\\n        except json.JSONDecodeError as e:\\n            raise ValueError(f\\"Invalid JSON in metadata file: {metadata_file}. object based off of `LLM` attributes.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            row: Input data to format into a prompt\\n            idx: Index of the row in the dataset\\n            generation_params_per_row: Whether the dataset has a generation_params column\\n        Returns:\\n            GenericRequest object containing the formatted request\\n\\n        Raises:\\n            ValueError: If prompt_func has invalid number of arguments or returns invalid format\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        # Convert BaseModel to dict for serialization\\n        if isinstance(row, BaseModel):\\n            row = row.model_dump()\\n        prompts = self.get_prompts(row)\\n        messages = self.get_messages(prompts)\\n        messages = self._put_system_prompt(messages)\\n        multimodal_prompt = isinstance(prompts, tuple)\\n\\n        row_generation_params = copy.deepcopy(self.generation_params)\\n        # Specify generation_params given in the row if applicable\\n        if generation_params_per_row:\\n            try:\\n                # Convert generation_params back to dict\\n                # Previously it was passed into the Dataset as a string to avoid automatic dictionary expansion\\n                # See https://github.com/bespokelabsai/curator/issues/325 for more detail\\n                loaded_params = json.loads(row[\\"generation_params\\"])\\n                # Update only the keys that exist in row-level generation_params\\n                row_generation_params.update(loaded_params)\\n            except json.JSONDecodeError:\\n                logger.warning(f\\"Failed to parse generation params as JSON: {row[\'generation_params\']}. Using default generation params.\\")\\n\\n        return GenericRequest(\\n            model=self.model_name,\\n            messages=messages,\\n            original_row=row,\\n            original_row_idx=idx,\\n            response_format=(self.response_format.model_json_schema() if self.response_format else None),\\n            is_multimodal_prompt=multimodal_prompt,\\n            generation_params=row_generation_params,\\n        )\\n\\n    def response_to_response_format(self, response_message: str | dict) -> Optional[dict | str]:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Converts a response message to a specified Pydantic model format.\\n\\n        This method takes a response message (either as a string or dict) and validates/converts it\\n        according to the provided Pydantic model format. If the response message is a string,\\n        it first attempts to parse it as JSON. The resulting dict is then used to construct\\n        an instance of the specified Pydantic model.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            response_message: The response message to convert, either as a JSON string\\n                or a dictionary.\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            The validated response message as a Pydantic model instance.\\n\\n        Raises:\\n            json.JSONDecodeError: If the response_message is a string but cannot be parsed as valid JSON.\\n            ValidationError: If the parsed response does not match the schema defined by response_format.\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        # Response message is a string, which is converted to a dict\\n        # The dict is then used to construct the response_format Pydantic model\\n        if self.response_format is None:\\n            return response_message\\n\\n        try:\\n            # First try to parse the response message as JSON\\n            if isinstance(response_message, str):\\n                try:\\n                    response_dict = json.loads(response_message)\\n                except json.JSONDecodeError as e:\\n                    logger.warning(f\\"Failed to parse response message as JSON: {response_message}. The model likely returned an invalid JSON format.\\")\\n                    raise e\\n            else:\\n                response_dict = response_message\\n\\n            # Then construct the Pydantic model from the parsed dict\\n            response_message = self.response_format(**response_dict)\\n            return response_message\\n\\n        except ValidationError as e:\\n            schema_str = json.dumps(self.response_format.model_json_schema(), indent=2)\\n            logger.warning(\\n                f\\"Pydantic failed to parse response message {response_message} with `response_format` {schema_str}. \\"\\n                f\\"The model likely returned a JSON that does not match the schema of the `response_format`.\\"\\n            )\\n            raise e\\n\\n    def parse_response_message(self, response_message: str) -> tuple[Optional[dict | str], Optional[list[str]]]:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Parse a response message from the model.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            response_message: Raw response string from the model\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            Tuple containing:\\n                - Parsed response message (as dict or str) or None if parsing failed\\n                - List of error messages if any occurred during parsing, or None\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        response_errors = None\\n        if self.response_format:\\n            try:\\n                response_message = json.loads(response_message)\\n            except json.JSONDecodeError:\\n                logger.warning(f\\"Failed to parse response as JSON: {response_message}, skipping this response.\\")\\n                response_message = None\\n                response_errors = [f\\"Failed to parse response as JSON: {response_message}\\"]\\n        return response_message, response_errors",\n  "error": "",\n  "base64_image": null,\n  "system": null\n}', annotations=None, meta=None)] structuredContent=None isError=False

Get the source code of both agents\' prompt functions\\n        seeder_prompt_func = _get_function_source(self.seeder.prompt_formatter.prompt_func)\\n        partner_prompt_func = _get_function_source(self.partner.prompt_formatter.prompt_func)\\n        seeder_parse_func = _get_function_source(self.seeder.prompt_formatter.parse_func)\\n        partner_parse_func = _get_function_source(self.partner.prompt_formatter.parse_func)\\n\\n        return {\\n            \\"run_hash\\": fingerprint,\\n            \\"dataset_hash\\": fingerprint,\\n            \\"prompt_func\\": f\\"Seeder ({self.seeder.name}):\\\\n{seeder_prompt_func}\\\\n\\\\nPartner ({self.partner.name}):\\\\n{partner_prompt_func}\\",\\n            \\"parse_func\\": f\\"Seeder ({self.seeder.name}):\\\\n{seeder_parse_func}\\\\n\\\\nPartner ({self.partner.name}):\\\\n{partner_parse_func}\\",\\n            \\"model_name\\": f\\"{self.seeder.prompt_formatter.model_name} + {self.partner.prompt_formatter.model_name}\\",\\n            \\"response_format\\": \\"N/A\\",\\n            \\"batch_mode\\": False,\\n            \\"status\\": \\"COMPLETED\\",\\n            \\"timestamp\\": datetime.now().isoformat(),\\n        }\\n\\n    def _handle_viewer_push(self, viewer_client: Client, dataset, metadata_dict: dict, working_dir: str) -> str | None:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Handle pushing the dataset to the curator viewer.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            viewer_client (Client): The curator viewer client.\\n            dataset: The dataset to push.\\n            metadata_dict (dict): Metadata for the viewer.\\n            working_dir (str): The working directory for this run.\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            str | None: The viewer URL if successful, None otherwise.\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        # Create or get existing session\\n        metadata_db_path = os.path.join(working_dir, \\"metadata.db\\")\\n        metadata_db = MetadataDB(metadata_db_path)\\n        existing_session_id = metadata_db.get_existing_session_id(metadata_dict[\\"run_hash\\"])\\n        existing_viewer_sync = metadata_db.check_existing_hosted_sync(metadata_dict[\\"run_hash\\"])\\n\\n        if not existing_viewer_sync and existing_session_id:\\n            session_id = viewer_client.create_session(metadata_dict)\\n        else:\\n            session_id = viewer_client.create_session(metadata_dict, session_id=existing_session_id)\\n\\n        metadata_dict[\\"session_id\\"] = session_id\\n        metadata_dict[\\"is_hosted_viewer_synced\\"] = False\\n        metadata_db.store_metadata(metadata_dict)\\n\\n        # Push dataset to viewer\\n        if existing_session_id is None:\\n            # No existing session, push the dataset\\n            push_to_viewer(dataset, session_id=session_id)\\n        else:\\n            # col[1] = column name\\n\\n        if set(current_columns) != set(expected_columns):\\n            msg = (\\n                \\"Detected a mismatch between the local DB schema and the expected schema. \\"\\n                \\"Please clear your cache with `rm -rf ~/.cache/curator` or \\"\\n                \\"`rm -rf $CURATOR_CACHE_DIR` if set.\\"\\n            )\\n            raise RuntimeError(msg)\\n\\n    def store_metadata(self, metadata: dict):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Store metadata about a Bella run in the database.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            metadata: Dictionary containing run metadata with keys:\\n                - timestamp: ISO format timestamp\\n                - dataset_hash: Unique hash of input dataset\\n                - code: Source code of code string function\\n                - code_input: Source code of test cases function\\n                - code_output: Source code of parse results function\\n                - run_hash: Unique hash identifying the run\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(self.db_path), exist_ok=True)\\n        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:\\n            cursor = conn.cursor()\\n            # IMPORTANT: If you modify the CREATE TABLE schema below,\\n            # you must update the expected_columns list in validate_schema()\\n            # to match the new schema. Otherwise, schema validation will fail.\\n            cursor.execute(\\n                \\"\\"\\"\\n                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS runs_code (\\n                    run_hash TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\\n                    dataset_hash TEXT,\\n                    code TEXT,\\n                    code_input TEXT,\\n                    code_output TEXT,\\n                    created_time TEXT,\\n                    last_edited_time TEXT\\n                )\\n                \\"\\"\\"\\n            )\\n            self.validate_schema()\\n\\n            # Check if run_hash exists\\n            cursor.execute(\\n                \\"SELECT run_hash FROM runs_code WHERE run_hash = ?\\",\\n                (metadata[\\"run_hash\\"],),\\n            )\\n            existing_run = cursor.fetchone()\\n\\n            if existing_run:\\n                # Insert new entry\\n                cursor.execute(\\n                    \\"\\"\\"\\n                    INSERT INTO runs_code (\\n                        run_hash, dataset_hash, code, code_input, code_output, created_time, last_edited_time\\n                    ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\\n                    \\"\\"\\",\\n                    (\\n                        metadata[\\"run_hash\\"],\\n                        metadata[\\"dataset_hash\\"],\\n                        metadata[\\"code\\"],\\n                        metadata[\\"code_input\\"],\\n                        metadata[\\"code_output\\"],\\n                        metadata[\\"timestamp\\"],\\n                        \\"-\\",\\n                    ),\\n                )\\n            conn.commit()",\n  "error": "",\n  "base64_image": null,\n  "system": null\n}', annotations=None, meta=None)] structuredContent=None isError=False

self.digest,\n            "components": self.components,\n            "created_at": self.created_at,\n            "updated_at": self.updated_at,\n        }\n\n    @classmethod\n    def from_dict(cls, outcome of ``reconcile_run_directory``.\n\n    Attributes:\n        status: One of ``"created"``, ``"adopted"``, ``"upgraded"`` or\n            ``"matched"``.\n        stamp: The ``RunStamp`` that is a match.\n    """\n\n    status: str\n    stamp: RunStamp\n    previous_version: Optional[int]\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Identity computation\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef _canonical_components(components: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:\n    """Serialise identity components in a canonical, key-sorted, version-tagged\n    form suitable for hashing."""\n    tagged = {\n        "identity_version": RUN_IDENTITY_VERSION,\n        "components": {k: components[k] for k in sorted(components)},\n    }\n    return json.dumps(tagged, sort_keys=True, default=str)\n\n\ndef compute_run_identity(\n    llm: Any,\n    dataset_hash: str,\n    *,\n    cache_enabled: bool = True,\n    **kwargs: Any,\n) -> RunIdentity:\n    """Compute a :class:`RunIdentity` from a duck-typed ``llm`` object.\n\n    The ``llm`` object is duck-typed: we only access attributes via\n    ``getattr`` and never import or ``isinstance``-check against ``LLM``. This\n    keeps the module free of import cycles.\n\n    The components that go into the digest are:\n\n    * ``dataset_hash`` - the input dataset fingerprint.\n    * ``prompt_func`` - hash of the prompt function.\n    * ``parse_func`` - hash of the parse function.\n    * ``model_name`` - the model name.\n    * ``response_format`` - the JSON schema of the response format, or\n      ``"text"`` when none.\n    * ``batch_mode`` - whether batch mode is in use.\n    * ``generation_params`` - the sorted items of the generation params dict.\n    * ``backend`` - the backend name.\n    * ``backend_params`` - the backend params dict (sorted by key).\n\n    Args:\n        llm: A duck-typed object exposing ``prompt_formatter``, ``batch_mode``\n            and a ``backend``/``backend_params`` property pair.\n        dataset_hash: The fingerprint of the input dataset.\n        cache_enabled: Whether caching is enabled. When ``False`` the\n            ``run_hash`` is random and the ``digest`` is empty.\n        **kwargs: Ignored, accepted so callers can pass extra keyword options\n            forward-compatibly.\n\n    Returns:\n        The computed :class:`RunIdentity`.\n    """\n    prompt_formatter = getattr(llm, "prompt_formatter", None)\n\n    prompt_func = getattr(prompt_formatter, "prompt_func", None)\n    parse_func = getattr(prompt_formatter, "parse_func", None)\n    model_name = getattr(prompt_formatter, "model_name", None)\n    response_format = getattr(prompt_formatter, "response_format", None)\n    generation_params = getattr(prompt_formatter, "generation_params", None) or {}\n\n    batch_mode = getattr(llm, "batch_mode", False)\n\n    # backend / backend_params are exposed as properties on LLM; fall back to\n    # the request processor if the object does not expose them directly.\n    backend = kwargs.get("backend")\n    if backend is None:\n        backend = getattr(llm, "backend", None)\n        if backend is None:\n            rp = getattr(llm, "_request_processor", None)\n            backend = getattr(rp, "backend", None) if rp is not None else None\n\n    backend_params = kwargs.get("backend_params")\n    if backend_params is None:\n        backend_params = getattr(llm, "backend_params", None)\n\n    response_format_value = "text"\n    if response_format is not None:\n        model_json_schema = getattr(response_format, "model_json_schema", None)\n        if callable(model_json_schema):\n            response_format_value = model_json_schema()\n        else:\n            response_format_value = response_format\n\n    components: Dict[str, Any] = {\n        "dataset_hash": str(dataset_hash),\n        "prompt_func": _get_function_hash(prompt_func),\n        "parse_func": _get_function_hash(parse_func),\n        "model_name": str(model_name),\n        "response_format": response_format_value,\n        "batch_mode": bool(batch_mode),\n        "generation_params": sorted(generation_params.items()),\n        "backend": str(backend),\n        "backend_params": dict(sorted((backend_params or {}).items())),\n    }\n\n    if not cache_enabled:\n        run_hash = xxh64(os.urandom(8)).hexdigest()\n        return RunIdentity(\n            run_hash=run_hash,\n            digest="",\n            identity_version=RUN_IDENTITY_VERSION,\n            cache_enabled=False,\n            components=components,\n        )\n\n    canonical = _canonical_components(components)\n    digest = xxh64(canonical.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()\n    run_hash = f"v{RUN_IDENTITY_VERSION}-{digest}"\n    logger.debug(f"Curator run identity components: {components}")\n    logger.debug(f"Curator run identity canonical: {canonical}")\n    logger.debug(f"Curator run identity digest: {digest}")\n    logger.debug(f"Curator run identity run_hash: {run_hash}")\n    return RunIdentity(\n        run_hash=run_hash,\n        digest=digest,\n        identity_version=RUN_IDENTITY_VERSION,\n        cache_enabled=True,\n        components=components,\n    )\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Stamp read/write\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef _stamp_path(run_cache_dir: str) -> str:\n    return os.path.join(run_cache_dir, RUN_IDENTITY_FILENAME)\n\n\ndef write_run_stamp(\n    run_cache_dir: str,\n    identity: RunIdentity,\n    *,\n    now: str,\n    created_at: Optional[str] = None,\n) -> RunStamp:\n    """Write a run stamp to ``run_cache_dir``.\n\n    The stamp file holds exactly ``identity_version``, ``run_hash``,\n    ``digest``, ``components``, ``created_at`` and ``updated_at`` as\n    ``json.dumps(..., indent=2, sort_keys=True)`` plus a trailing newline.\n    ``created_at`` is preserved when supplied (e.g. during an upgrade), and\n    ``updated_at`` is always set to ``now``.\n\n    Args:\n        run_cache_dir: The run cache directory to stamp.\n        identity: The run identity to record.\n        now: ISO-format timestamp for ``updated_at``.\n        created_at: Optional ISO-format timestamp to preserve as\n            ``created_at``; defaults to ``now``.\n\n    Returns:\n        The :class:`RunStamp` that was written.\n    """\n    os.makedirs(run_cache_dir, exist_ok=True)\n    stamp = RunStamp(\n        identity_version=identity.identity_version,\n        run_hash=identity.run_hash,\n        digest=identity.digest,\n        components=identity.components,\n        created_at=created_at if created_at is not None else now,\n        updated_at=now,\n    )\n    payload = json.dumps(stamp.to_dict(), indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\\n"\n    with open(_stamp_path(run_cache_dir), "w") as f:\n        f.write(payload)\n    return stamp\n\n\ndef read_run_stamp(run_cache_dir: str) -> Optional[RunStamp]:\n    """Read a run stamp from ``run_cache_dir``.\n\n    Returns ``None`` when the stamp file is absent, unparseable or incomplete\n    (i.e. missing any of the required keys).\n    """\n    path = _stamp_path(run_cache_dir)\n    if not os.path.exists(path):\n        return None\n    try:\n        with open(path, "r") as f:\n            data = json.load(f)\n    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError):\n        return None\n    if not isinstance(data, dict):\n        return None\n    required = (\n        "identity_version",\n        "run_hash",\n        "digest",\n        "components",\n        "created_at",\n        "updated_at",\n    )\n    if not all(key in data for key in required):\n        return None\n    try:\n        return RunStamp.from_dict(data)\n    except (KeyError, TypeError):\n        return None\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Reconciliation\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef _diff_components(\n    expected: Dict[str, Any],\n    found: Dict[str, Any],\n) -> Tuple[str, ...]:\n    """Return the alphabetically sorted tuple of component keys that differ\n    between ``expected`` and ``found``."""\n    keys = set(expected) | set(found)\n    mismatched = [k for k in keys if expected.get(k) != found.get(k)]\n    return tuple(sorted(mismatched))\n\n\ndef reconcile_run_directory(\n    run_cache_dir: str,\n    identity: RunIdentity,\n    *,\n    now: str,\n) -> RunDirectoryCheck:\n    """Reconcile a run cache directory against ``identity``.\n\n    The directory may be in one of several states:\n\n    * **missing or empty** -> ``status="created"``: create the directory and\n      write a fresh stamp. ``previous_version`` is ``None``.\n    * **non-empty but unstamped** -> ``status="adopted"``: write a fresh stamp.\n      ``previous_version`` is ``None``.\n    * **stamped with an older ``identity_version``** -> ``status="upgraded"``:\n      rewrite the stamp keeping its ``created_at``. ``previous_version`` is the\n      old integer version.\n    * **stamped with the current ``identity_version`` and matching identity**\n      -> ``status="matched"``: refresh ``updated_at`` keeping ``created_at``.\n      ``previous_version`` is ``RUN_IDENTITY_VERSION``.\n    * **stamped with the current ``identity_version`` but a disagreeing\n      identity**, or **a newer ``identity_version``** -> raise\n      :class:`RunIdentityMismatch` without deleting anything.\n\n    Args:\n        run_cache_dir: The run cache directory to reconcile.\n        identity: The current run identity.\n        now: ISO-format timestamp.\n\n    Returns:\n        A :class:`RunDirectoryCheck` describing what happened.\n\n    Raises:\n        RunIdentityMismatch: When a same-version stamp disagrees or a newer\n            ``identity_version`` stamp is found.\n    """\n    stamp = read_run_stamp(run_cache_dir)\n    exists = os.path.isdir(run_cache_dir)\n    non_empty = exists and bool(os.listdir(run_cache_dir)) if exists else False\n\n    if stamp is None:\n        # Missing/empty directory or an unstamped one.\n        if not exists or not non_empty:\n            # create + stamp\n            new_stamp = write_run_stamp(run_cache_dir, identity, now=now)\n            return RunDirectoryCheck(status="created", stamp=new_stamp, previous_version=None)\n        # adopt: non-empty, unstamped directory.\n        new_stamp = write_run_stamp(run_cache_dir, identity, now=now)\n        return RunDirectoryCheck(status="adopted", stamp=new_stamp, previous_version=None)\n\n    # We have a stamp.\n    if stamp.identity_version < RUN_IDENTITY_VERSION:\n        # upgrade: rewrite keeping created_at.\n        new_stamp = write_run_stamp(\n            run_cache_dir, identity, now=now, created_at=stamp.created_at\n        )\n        return RunDirectoryCheck(\n            status="upgraded", stamp=new_stamp, previous_version=stamp.identity_version\n        )\n\n    if stamp.identity_version > RUN_IDENTITY_VERSION:\n        # Newer than we know about; refuse.\n        raise RunIdentityMismatch(\n            path=run_cache_dir,\n            expected_run_hash=identity.run_hash,\n            found_run_hash=stamp.run_hash,\n            mismatched_components=("identity_version",),\n        )\n\n    # Same version: compare identities.\n    if stamp.run_hash == identity.run_hash and stamp.digest == identity.digest:\n        # exact match: refresh updated_at keeping created_at.\n        new_stamp = write_run_stamp(\n            run_cache_dir, identity, now=now, created_at=stamp.created_at\n        )\n        return RunDirectoryCheck(\n            status="matched", stamp=new_stamp, previous_version=RUN_IDENTITY_VERSION\n        )\n\n    # Same version but disagreeing identity.\n    mismatched = _diff_components(identity.components, stamp.components)\n    if not mismatched:\n        # Shouldn\'t happen given run_hash differs, but be safe.\n        mismatched = ("run_hash",)\n    raise RunIdentityMismatch(\n        path=run_cache_dir,\n        expected_run_hash=identity.run_hash,\n        found_run_hash=stamp.run_hash,\n        mismatched_components=mismatched,\n    )\n'}

Check if run_hash exists\n            cursor.execute(\n                "SELECT run_hash FROM runs WHERE run_hash = ?",\n                (metadata["run_hash"],),\n            )\n            existing_run = cursor.fetchone()\n\n            if existing_run:\n                # Update last_edited_time for existing entry\n                cursor.execute(\n                    """\n                    UPDATE runs\n                    SET last_edited_time = ?\n                    WHERE run_hash = ?\n                    """,\n                    (metadata["timestamp"], metadata["run_hash"]),\n                )\n            else:\n                # str_replace_editor: {'command': 'str_replace', 'new_str': '    @classmethod\n    def load(cls, cache_dir: Union[str, Path], dataset: Dataset, *, verify: bool = True) -> "CuratorResponse":\n        """Load a response from a cache directory.\n\n        This class method creates a new CuratorResponse instance from saved\n        data in the cache directory. It requires a dataset to be provided\n        as the dataset itself is not cached.\n\n        When ``verify`` is ``True`` (the default) the recorded ``"dataset"``\n        block (``fingerprint``, ``size``, ``columns``) is checked against the\n        dataset handed in, raising :class:`CachedResponseMismatch` on the\n        first differing field. The check is skipped when the ``"dataset"`` key\n        is absent in the cached ``response.json`` or when ``verify`` is\n        ``False``.\n\n        Args:\n            cache_dir (Union[str, Path]): Directory containing the cached response data.\n            dataset (Dataset): The dataset to use for the response.\n            verify (bool): Whether to verify the cached dataset block against\n                the provided dataset. Defaults to ``True``.\n\n        Returns:\n            CuratorResponse: A new CuratorResponse instance loaded from cache.\n\n        Raises:\n            CachedResponseMismatch: If ``verify`` is ``True`` and the recorded\n                dataset block disagrees with the provided dataset.\n        """\n        cache_dir = Path(cache_dir)\n\n        # Load response.json\n        with open(cache_dir / "response.json", "r") as f:\n            data = json.load(f)\n\n        # Verify the recorded dataset block against the dataset we were\n        # handed, raising on the first differing field.\n        if verify and isinstance(data.get("dataset"), dict):\n            recorded = data["dataset"]\n            expected = {\n                "fingerprint": dataset._fingerprint,\n                "size": len(dataset),\n                "columns": list(dataset.column_names),\n            }\n            for field_name in ("fingerprint", "size", "columns"):\n                if field_name in recorded and recorded.get(field_name) != expected[field_name]:\n                    raise CachedResponseMismatch(\n                        cache_dir=str(cache_dir),\n                        field=field_name,\n                        expected=expected[field_name],\n                        found=recorded.get(field_name),\n                    )\n\n        # Create response object\n        response = cls(\n            dataset=dataset,\n            failed_requests_path=cache_dir / "failed_requests.jsonl" if (cache_dir / "failed_requests.jsonl").exists() else None,\n            model_name=data["model_name"],\n            max_requests_per_minute=data["max_requests_per_minute"],\n            max_tokens_per_minute=data["max_tokens_per_minute"],\n            token_usage=TokenUsage(**data["token_usage"]),\n            cost_info=CostInfo(**data["cost_info"]),\n            request_stats=RequestStats(**data["request_stats"]),\n            performance_stats=PerformanceStats(**data["performance_stats"]),\n            metadata=data["metadata"],\n            run_identity=data.get("run_identity"),\n        )\n\n        return response', 'old_str': '    @classmethod\n    def load(cls, cache_dir: Union[str, Path], dataset: Dataset) -> "CuratorResponse":\n        """Load a response from a cache directory.\n\n        This class method creates a new CuratorResponse instance from saved\n        data in the cache directory. match\n    check2 = reconcile_run_directory(run_dir, ident, now=\'2024-01-01T00:01:00\')\n    print(\'status2\', check2.status, \'prev2\', check2.previous_version)\n    assert check2.status == \'matched\'\n    assert check2.previous_version == RUN_IDENTITY_VERSION\n    stamp = read_run_stamp(run_dir)\n    assert stamp.created_at == \'2024-01-01T00:00:00\'\n    assert stamp.updated_at == \'2024-01-01T00:01:00\'\n    print(\'match ok, created_at preserved:\', stamp.created_at, \'updated:\', stamp.updated_at)\n\n    # mismatch\n    ident2 = compute_run_identity(llm, \'different_hash\')\n    run_dir2 = os.path.join(d, ident2.run_hash)\n    reconcile_run_directory(run_dir2, ident2, now=\'2024-01-01T00:02:00\')\n    try:\n        reconcile_run_directory(run_dir2, ident, now=\'2024-01-01T00:03:00\')\n        print(\'FAIL: should have raised\')\n    except RunIdentityMismatch as e:\n        print(\'mismatch raised, components:\', e.mismatched_components)\n        assert \'dataset_hash\' in e.mismatched_components\n\n# adopt: non-empty unstamped dir\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:\n    run_dir = os.path.join(d, ident.run_hash)\n    os.makedirs(run_dir)\n    with open(os.path.join(run_dir, \'requests_0.jsonl\'), \'w\') as f:\n        f.write(\'{}\\n\')\n    check = reconcile_run_directory(run_dir, ident, now=\'2024-01-01T00:00:00\')\n    print(\'adopt status\', check.status, \'prev\', check.previous_version)\n    assert check.status == \'adopted\'\n    assert check.previous_version is None\n\n# upgrade: older version stamp\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:\n    run_dir = os.path.join(d, ident.run_hash)\n    os.makedirs(run_dir)\n    old_stamp = {\'identity_version\': 2, \'run_hash\': \'old\', \'digest\': \'olddigest\', \'components\': {}, \'created_at\': \'2023-01-01T00:00:00\', \'updated_at\': \'2023-01-01T00:00:00\'}\n    with open(os.path.join(run_dir, RUN_IDENTITY_FILENAME), \'w\') as f:\n        json.dump(old_stamp, f)\n    check = reconcile_run_directory(run_dir, ident, now=\'2024-01-01T00:00:00\')\n    print(\'upgrade status\', check.status, \'prev\', check.previous_version)\n    assert check.status == \'upgraded\'\n    assert check.previous_version == 2\n    stamp = read_run_stamp(run_dir)\n    assert stamp.created_at == \'2023-01-01T00:00:00\'  # preserved\n    assert stamp.identity_version == RUN_IDENTITY_VERSION\n\n# newer version: raise\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:\n    run_dir = os.path.join(d, ident.run_hash)\n    os.makedirs(run_dir)\n    new_stamp = {\'identity_version\': RUN_IDENTITY_VERSION+1, \'run_hash\': \'new\', \'digest\': \'newd\', \'components\': {}, \'created_at\': \'2023-01-01T00:00:00\', \'updated_at\': \'2023-01-01T00:00:00\'}\n    with open(os.path.join(run_dir, RUN_IDENTITY_FILENAME), \'w\') as f:\n        json.dump(new_stamp, f)\n    try:\n        reconcile_run_directory(run_dir, ident, now=\'2024-01-01T00:00:00\')\n        print(\'FAIL newer should raise\')\n    except RunIdentityMismatch as e:\n        print(\'newer raised, components:\', e.mismatched_components)\n        assert e.mismatched_components == (\'identity_version\',)\n\n# cache disabled\nident_d = compute_run_identity(llm, \'dataset_hash_123\', cache_enabled=False)\nprint(\'disabled run_hash\', ident_d.run_hash, \'digest\', repr(ident_d.digest))\nassert ident_d.digest == \'\'\nassert ident_d.cache_enabled is False\n\n# read_run_stamp None cases\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:\n    empty = os.path.join(d, \'empty\')\n    os.makedirs(empty)\n    assert read_run_stamp(empty) is None  # absent\n    with open(os.path.join(empty, RUN_IDENTITY_FILENAME), \'w\') as f:\n        f.write(\'not json{\')\n    assert read_run_stamp(empty) is None  # unparseable\n    with open(os.path.join(empty, RUN_IDENTITY_FILENAME), \'w\') as f:\n        json.dump({\'identity_version\': 3}, f)\n    assert read_run_stamp(empty) is None  # incomplete\n\nprint(\'ALL RUN_IDENTITY TESTS PASSED\')\n" 2>&1 | tail -30'}
